{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NTjLNNK6hy8cfoegRJmR5j","type":"tool_result","content":"256\t  async getDataRequest(id: string): Promise<DataRequest> {\n257\t    const request = await this.dataRequestRepository.findOne({ where: { id } });\n258\t    if (!request) {\n259\t      throw new NotFoundException(`Data request not found: ${id}`);\n260\t    }\n261\t    return request;\n262\t  }\n263\t\n264\t  /**\n265\t   * Get all data requests with filters\n266\t   */\n267\t  async getDataRequests(options: {\n268\t    page?: number;\n269\t    limit?: number;\n270\t    tenantId?: string;\n271\t    requestType?: DataRequestType;\n272\t    status?: DataRequestStatus;\n273\t    complianceFramework?: ComplianceType;\n274\t    startDate?: Date;\n275\t    endDate?: Date;\n276\t    overdue?: boolean;\n277\t  }): Promise<PaginationResultV1<DataRequest>> {\n278\t    const {\n279\t      page = 1,\n280\t      limit = 20,\n281\t      tenantId,\n282\t      requestType,\n283\t      status,\n284\t      complianceFramework,\n285\t      startDate,\n286\t      endDate,\n287\t      overdue,\n288\t    } = options;\n289\t\n290\t    const qb = this.dataRequestRepository.createQueryBuilder('request');\n291\t\n292\t    if (tenantId) qb.andWhere('request.tenantId = :tenantId', { tenantId });\n293\t    if (requestType) qb.andWhere('request.requestType = :requestType', { requestType });\n294\t    if (status) qb.andWhere('request.status = :status', { status });\n295\t    if (complianceFramework) qb.andWhere('request.complianceFramework = :complianceFramework', { complianceFramework });\n296\t    if (startDate) qb.andWhere('request.createdAt >= :startDate', { startDate });\n297\t    if (endDate) qb.andWhere('request.createdAt <= :endDate', { endDate });\n298\t    if (overdue) {\n299\t      qb.andWhere('request.dueDate < :now', { now: new Date() });\n300\t      qb.andWhere('request.status NOT IN (:...completedStatuses)', {\n301\t        completedStatuses: ['completed', 'rejected'],\n302\t      });\n303\t    }\n304\t\n305\t    qb.orderBy('request.dueDate', 'ASC');\n306\t    qb.skip((page - 1) * limit);\n307\t    qb.take(limit);\n308\t\n309\t    const [data, total] = await qb.getManyAndCount();\n310\t\n311\t    return createStandardPaginatedResult<DataRequest>(data, total, page, limit);\n312\t  }\n313\t\n314\t  /**\n315\t   * Update data request\n316\t   */\n317\t  async updateDataRequest(\n318\t    id: string,\n319\t    data: Partial<{\n320\t      status: DataRequestStatus;\n321\t      assignedTo: string;\n322\t      assignedToName: string;\n323\t      completionNotes: string;\n324\t      rejectionReason: string;\n325\t    }>,\n326\t    actorId: string,\n327\t    actorName: string,\n328\t  ): Promise<DataRequest> {\n329\t    const request = await this.getDataRequest(id);\n330\t\n331\t    // Track changes in audit trail\n332\t    const auditEntry = {\n333\t      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n334\t      action: this.getStatusChangeDescription(request.status, data.status),\n335\t      actor: actorName,\n336\t      details: data.completionNotes || data.rejectionReason,\n337\t    };\n338\t\n339\t    if (!request.auditTrail) request.auditTrail = [];\n340\t    request.auditTrail.push(auditEntry);\n341\t\n342\t    // Update fields\n343\t    if (data.status) {\n344\t      request.status = data.status;\n345\t      if (data.status === 'in_progress' && !request.processingStartedAt) {\n346\t        request.processingStartedAt = new Date();\n347\t      }\n348\t      if (data.status === 'completed') {\n349\t        request.completedAt = new Date();\n350\t        request.completedBy = actorId;\n351\t      }\n352\t    }\n353\t\n354\t    if (data.assignedTo) {\n355\t      request.assignedTo = data.assignedTo;\n356\t      request.assignedToName = data.assignedToName || null;\n357\t    }\n358\t\n359\t    if (data.completionNotes) {\n360\t      request.completionNotes = data.completionNotes;\n361\t    }\n362\t\n363\t    if (data.rejectionReason) {\n364\t      request.rejectionReason = data.rejectionReason;\n365\t    }\n366\t\n367\t    return this.dataRequestRepository.save(request);\n368\t  }\n369\t\n370\t  /**\n371\t   * Verify requester identity\n372\t   */\n373\t  async verifyIdentity(\n374\t    id: string,\n375\t    verifiedBy: string,\n376\t    verificationMethod: string,\n377\t  ): Promise<DataRequest> {\n378\t    const request = await this.getDataRequest(id);\n379\t\n380\t    request.identityVerified = true;\n381\t    request.verifiedAt = new Date();\n382\t    request.verifiedBy = verifiedBy;\n383\t    request.verificationMethod = verificationMethod;\n384\t\n385\t    request.auditTrail?.push({\n386\t      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n387\t      action: 'Identity verified',\n388\t      actor: verifiedBy,\n389\t      details: `Verification method: ${verificationMethod}`,\n390\t    });\n391\t\n392\t    return this.dataRequestRepository.save(request);\n393\t  }\n394\t\n395\t  /**\n396\t   * Complete data request with delivery\n397\t   */\n398\t  async completeDataRequest(\n399\t    id: string,\n400\t    params: {\n401\t      completedBy: string;\n402\t      completionNotes: string;\n403\t      deliveryFormat?: 'json' | 'csv' | 'pdf' | 'xml';\n404\t      downloadUrl?: string;\n405\t      downloadExpiresAt?: Date;\n406\t    },\n407\t  ): Promise<DataRequest> {\n408\t    const request = await this.getDataRequest(id);\n409\t\n410\t    if (request.status === 'completed') {\n411\t      throw new BadRequestException('Request is already completed');\n412\t    }\n413\t\n414\t    request.status = 'completed';\n415\t    request.completedAt = new Date();\n416\t    request.completedBy = params.completedBy;\n417\t    request.completionNotes = params.completionNotes;\n418\t\n419\t    if (params.deliveryFormat) {\n420\t      request.deliveryFormat = params.deliveryFormat;\n421\t      request.downloadUrl = params.downloadUrl || null;\n422\t      request.downloadExpiresAt = params.downloadExpiresAt || null;\n423\t    }\n424\t\n425\t    request.auditTrail?.push({\n426\t      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n427\t      action: 'Request completed',\n428\t      actor: params.completedBy,\n429\t      details: params.completionNotes,\n430\t    });\n431\t\n432\t    return this.dataRequestRepository.save(request);\n433\t  }\n434\t\n435\t  /**\n436\t   * Record download of data request\n437\t   */\n438\t  async recordDownload(id: string): Promise<void> {\n439\t    await this.dataRequestRepository.increment({ id }, 'downloadCount', 1);\n440\t  }\n441\t\n442\t  /**\n443\t   * Get overdue requests\n444\t   */\n445\t  async getOverdueRequests(): Promise<DataRequest[]> {\n446\t    return this.dataRequestRepository.find({\n447\t      where: {\n448\t        dueDate: LessThan(new Date()),\n449\t        status: In(['pending', 'in_progress']),\n450\t      },\n451\t      order: { dueDate: 'ASC' },\n452\t    });\n453\t  }\n454\t\n455\t  /**\n456\t   * Get request statistics\n457\t   */\n458\t  async getDataRequestStats(options: {\n459\t    tenantId?: string;\n460\t    startDate?: Date;\n461\t    endDate?: Date;\n462\t  }): Promise<{\n463\t    total: number;\n464\t    byStatus: Record<DataRequestStatus, number>;\n465\t    byType: Record<DataRequestType, number>;\n466\t    avgResponseDays: number;\n467\t    overdueCount: number;\n468\t    completionRate: number;\n469\t  }> {\n470\t    const { tenantId, startDate, endDate } = options;\n471\t\n472\t    const qb = this.dataRequestRepository.createQueryBuilder('request');\n473\t    if (tenantId) qb.andWhere('request.tenantId = :tenantId', { tenantId });\n474\t    if (startDate) qb.andWhere('request.createdAt >= :startDate', { startDate });\n475\t    if (endDate) qb.andWhere('request.createdAt <= :endDate', { endDate });\n476\t\n477\t    const all = await qb.getMany();\n478\t\n479\t    // By status\n480\t    const byStatus = {} as Record<DataRequestStatus, number>;\n481\t    const statuses: DataRequestStatus[] = ['pending', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'rejected', 'expired'];\n482\t    statuses.forEach((s) => {\n483\t      byStatus[s] = all.filter((r) => r.status === s).length;\n484\t    });\n485\t\n486\t    // By type\n487\t    const byType = {} as Record<DataRequestType, number>;\n488\t    const types: DataRequestType[] = ['access', 'deletion', 'portability', 'rectification', 'restriction'];\n489\t    types.forEach((t) => {\n490\t      byType[t] = all.filter((r) => r.requestType === t).length;\n491\t    });\n492\t\n493\t    // Average response time\n494\t    const completedWithTime = all.filter((r) => r.completedAt && r.createdAt);\n495\t    const avgResponseDays = completedWithTime.length > 0\n496\t      ? completedWithTime.reduce((sum, r) => {\n497\t          const days = (r.completedAt!.getTime() - r.createdAt.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);\n498\t          return sum + days;\n499\t        }, 0) / completedWithTime.length\n500\t      : 0;\n501\t\n502\t    // Overdue count\n503\t    const now = new Date();\n504\t    const overdueCount = all.filter(\n505\t      (r) => r.dueDate < now && !['completed', 'rejected'].includes(r.status),\n506\t    ).length;\n507\t\n508\t    // Completion rate\n509\t    const completionRate = all.length > 0\n510\t      ? (all.filter((r) => r.status === 'completed').length / all.length) * 100\n511\t      : 0;\n512\t\n513\t    return {\n514\t      total: all.length,\n515\t      byStatus,\n516\t      byType,\n517\t      avgResponseDays: Math.round(avgResponseDays * 10) / 10,\n518\t      overdueCount,\n519\t      completionRate: Math.round(completionRate * 10) / 10,\n520\t    };\n521\t  }\n522\t\n523\t  // ============================================================================\n524\t  // Compliance Reporting\n525\t  // ============================================================================\n526\t\n527\t  /**\n528\t   * Generate compliance report\n529\t   */\n530\t  async generateComplianceReport(params: {\n531\t    complianceType: ComplianceType;\n532\t    reportPeriodStart: Date;\n533\t    reportPeriodEnd: Date;\n534\t    includedTenants?: string[];\n535\t    generatedBy: string;\n536\t    generatedByName: string;\n537\t    isAutoGenerated?: boolean;\n538\t  }): Promise<ComplianceReport> {\n539\t    this.logger.log(`Generating ${params.complianceType} compliance report`);\n540\t\n541\t    // Gather metrics\n542\t    const requestStats = await this.getDataRequestStats({\n543\t      startDate: params.reportPeriodStart,\n544\t      endDate: params.reportPeriodEnd,\n545\t    });\n546\t\n547\t    const incidents = await this.incidentRepository.count({\n548\t      where: {\n549\t        createdAt: Between(params.reportPeriodStart, params.reportPeriodEnd),\n550\t      },\n551\t    });\n552\t\n553\t    const dataBreaches = await this.incidentRepository.count({\n554\t      where: {\n555\t        dataBreached: true,\n556\t        createdAt: Between(params.reportPeriodStart, params.reportPeriodEnd),\n557\t      },\n558\t    });\n559\t\n560\t    // Run compliance checks\n561\t    const complianceResults = await this.runComplianceChecks(params.complianceType);\n562\t    const violations = complianceResults\n563\t      .filter((r) => r.status === 'non_compliant')\n564\t      .map((r) => ({\n565\t        requirement: r.requirement.requirement,\n566\t        description: r.details,\n567\t        severity: 'high' as const,\n568\t        remediation: r.remediation || 'Review and address non-compliance',\n569\t      }));\n570\t\n571\t    const complianceScore = this.calculateComplianceScore(complianceResults);\n572\t\n573\t    // Generate recommendations\n574\t    const recommendations = this.generateRecommendations(complianceResults, requestStats);\n575\t\n576\t    const report = this.reportRepository.create({\n577\t      title: `${params.complianceType.toUpperCase()} Compliance Report - ${this.formatPeriod(params.reportPeriodStart, params.reportPeriodEnd)}`,\n578\t      complianceType: params.complianceType,\n579\t      reportPeriodStart: params.reportPeriodStart,\n580\t      reportPeriodEnd: params.reportPeriodEnd,\n581\t      includedTenants: params.includedTenants || null,\n582\t      includesAllTenants: !params.includedTenants || params.includedTenants.length === 0,\n583\t      totalDataRequests: requestStats.total,\n584\t      completedDataRequests: requestStats.byStatus.completed || 0,\n585\t      pendingDataRequests: (requestStats.byStatus.pending || 0) + (requestStats.byStatus.in_progress || 0),\n586\t      avgResponseTimeDays: requestStats.avgResponseDays,\n587\t      securityIncidents: incidents,\n588\t      dataBreaches,\n589\t      complianceScore,\n590\t      violations: violations.length > 0 ? violations : null,\n591\t      recommendations: recommendations.length > 0 ? recommendations : null,\n592\t      executiveSummary: this.generateExecutiveSummary(params.complianceType, complianceScore, violations.length),\n593\t      detailedFindings: {\n594\t        complianceResults,\n595\t        requestStats,\n596\t        incidentSummary: { total: incidents, breaches: dataBreaches },\n597\t      },\n598\t      generatedBy: params.generatedBy,\n599\t      generatedByName: params.generatedByName,\n600\t      isAutoGenerated: params.isAutoGenerated ?? false,\n601\t    });\n602\t\n603\t    return this.reportRepository.save(report);\n604\t  }\n605\t\n606\t  /**\n607\t   * Get compliance report by ID\n608\t   */\n609\t  async getComplianceReport(id: string): Promise<ComplianceReport> {\n610\t    const report = await this.reportRepository.findOne({ where: { id } });\n611\t    if (!report) {\n612\t      throw new NotFoundException(`Compliance report not found: ${id}`);\n613\t    }\n614\t    return report;\n615\t  }\n616\t\n617\t  /**\n618\t   * Get all compliance reports\n619\t   */\n620\t  async getComplianceReports(options: {\n621\t    page?: number;\n622\t    limit?: number;\n623\t    complianceType?: ComplianceType;\n624\t    startDate?: Date;\n625\t    endDate?: Date;\n626\t  }): Promise<PaginationResultV1<ComplianceReport>> {\n627\t    const { page = 1, limit = 20, complianceType, startDate, endDate } = options;\n628\t\n629\t    const qb = this.reportRepository.createQueryBuilder('report');\n630\t\n631\t    if (complianceType) qb.andWhere('report.complianceType = :complianceType', { complianceType });\n632\t    if (startDate) qb.andWhere('report.reportPeriodStart >= :startDate', { startDate });\n633\t    if (endDate) qb.andWhere('report.reportPeriodEnd <= :endDate', { endDate });\n634\t\n635\t    qb.orderBy('report.createdAt', 'DESC');\n636\t    qb.skip((page - 1) * limit);\n637\t    qb.take(limit);\n638\t\n639\t    const [data, total] = await qb.getManyAndCount();\n640\t\n641\t    return createStandardPaginatedResult<ComplianceReport>(data, total, page, limit);\n642\t  }\n643\t\n644\t  /**\n645\t   * Run compliance checks for a framework\n646\t   */\n647\t  async runComplianceChecks(framework: ComplianceType): Promise<ComplianceCheckResult[]> {\n648\t    const requirements = this.getRequirements(framework);\n649\t    const results: ComplianceCheckResult[] = [];\n650\t\n651\t    for (const req of requirements) {\n652\t      const outcome = await this.checkRequirement(req);\n653\t      // Stamped per check, not per run: the checks execute sequentially and\n654\t      // some of them query, so one timestamp for the batch would be a claim\n655\t      // about when the last one finished.\n656\t      results.push({ ...outcome, checkedAt: new Date().toISOString() });\n657\t    }\n658\t\n659\t    return results;\n660\t  }\n661\t\n662\t  /**\n663\t   * Get compliance requirements for a framework\n664\t   */\n665\t  getRequirements(framework: ComplianceType): ComplianceRequirement[] {\n666\t    switch (framework) {\n667\t      case 'gdpr':\n668\t        return GDPR_REQUIREMENTS;\n669\t      default:\n670\t        // Frameworks beyond GDPR (KVKK, SOC 2, ISO 27001) currently\n671\t        // share the GDPR requirement set as their baseline because\n672\t        // the platform's automated checks cover GDPR-equivalent\n673\t        // surfaces. Distinct framework-specific requirement sets\n674\t        // are tracked under each framework's own ADR (per the\n675\t        // compliance-expert agent's framework-coverage matrix).\n676\t        return GDPR_REQUIREMENTS;\n677\t    }\n678\t  }\n679\t\n680\t  /**\n681\t   * Check a single requirement.\n682\t   *\n683\t   * # Why the default branch returns 'partial' (COMPLIANCE-MEDIUM-002 cure)\n684\t   *\n685\t   * Pre-cure the `default:` branch returned `status: 'compliant'`\n686\t   * for every requirement that did not have an explicit\n687\t   * automatable check (gdpr-1, gdpr-4, gdpr-5, gdpr-6, gdpr-7,\n688\t   * gdpr-8). The compliance score (`calculateComplianceScore`)\n689\t   * averages 'compliant' as 100% — so 6 of the 8 GDPR\n690\t   * requirements were inflating the score with unconditional\n691\t   * 100s, producing a misleading 88% headline that an auditor\n692\t   * would interpret as evidence of substantive automated\n693\t   * coverage.\n694\t   *\n695\t   * The compliance-expert agent's tier hierarchy permits Tier-4\n696\t   * (manual-attestation) checks but they MUST surface as\n697\t   * `status: 'partial'` with an explicit attestation pointer —\n698\t   * not as `'compliant'`. This cure restores tier-honesty:\n699\t   *\n700\t   *   - gdpr-2, gdpr-3 keep their automated checks (Tier-3).\n701\t   *   - The other six requirements fall through to `'partial'`\n702\t   *     with `details` pointing at the manual-attestation\n703\t   *     evidence path. The 50%-weighted partial score deflates\n704\t   *     the overall percentage to reflect actual automated\n705\t   *     coverage rather than fictitious passes.\n706\t   *\n707\t   * The `calculateComplianceScore` weights are unchanged:\n708\t   * compliant=100, partial=50, non_compliant=0, not_applicable=100.\n709\t   * The cure is in WHICH bucket the default branch reports.\n710\t   *\n711\t   * Operators wanting a fully-automated coverage view should\n712\t   * either implement Tier-3 checks for the remaining six (one\n713\t   * per ADR / domain owner), or build an attestation pipeline\n714\t   * that explicitly elevates each `partial` to `compliant`\n715\t   * upon evidence-document review.\n716\t   */\n717\t  private async checkRequirement(req: ComplianceRequirement): Promise<ComplianceCheckOutcome> {\n718\t    switch (req.id) {\n719\t      case 'gdpr-2': { // Data Subject Rights\n720\t        const pendingRequests = await this.dataRequestRepository.count({\n721\t          where: { status: In(['pending', 'in_progress']) },\n722\t        });\n723\t        const overdue = await this.getOverdueRequests();\n724\t        if (overdue.length > 0) {\n725\t          return {\n726\t            requirement: req,\n727\t            status: 'non_compliant',\n728\t            details: `${overdue.length} overdue data requests`,\n729\t            remediation: 'Process overdue requests immediately',\n730\t          };\n731\t        }\n732\t        return {\n733\t          requirement: req,\n734\t          status: 'compliant',\n735\t          details: `Data request handling operational. ${pendingRequests} pending requests.`,\n736\t        };\n737\t      }\n738\t\n739\t      case 'gdpr-3': { // Breach Notification\n740\t        const recentBreaches = await this.incidentRepository.count({\n741\t          where: {\n742\t            dataBreached: true,\n743\t            reportedToAuthorities: false,\n744\t            createdAt: MoreThan(new Date(Date.now() - 72 * 60 * 60 * 1000)), // Last 72 hours\n745\t          },\n746\t        });\n747\t        if (recentBreaches > 0) {\n748\t          return {\n749\t            requirement: req,\n750\t            status: 'non_compliant',\n751\t            details: `${recentBreaches} unreported data breach(es) within 72 hours`,\n752\t            remediation: 'Report breaches to supervisory authority immediately',\n753\t          };\n754\t        }\n755\t        return {\n756\t          requirement: req,\n757\t          status: 'compliant',\n758\t          details: 'No unreported breaches',\n759\t        };\n760\t      }\n761\t\n762\t      default:\n763\t        // COMPLIANCE-MEDIUM-002 cure: requirements without an\n764\t        // automated Tier-3 check report as 'partial' (not\n765\t        // 'compliant') so the score honestly reflects automated\n766\t        // coverage. `details` points at the per-requirement\n767\t        // attestation path operators must populate to elevate\n768\t        // the row to compliant — keeps the cure cooperative\n769\t        // with the future evidence-document pipeline.\n770\t        return {\n771\t          requirement: req,\n772\t          status: 'partial',\n773\t          details:\n774\t            `Automated check not implemented for requirement '${req.id}'. ` +\n775\t            `Manual attestation required — see ` +\n776\t            `docs/compliance/evidence/${req.id}.md.`,\n777\t          remediation:\n778\t            `Either implement an automated Tier-3 check for '${req.id}' ` +\n779\t            `in checkRequirement(), or populate the attestation document ` +\n780\t            `at docs/compliance/evidence/${req.id}.md and elevate via ` +\n781\t            `the evidence-review pipeline.`,\n782\t        };\n783\t    }\n784\t  }\n785\t\n786\t  /**\n787\t   * Calculate compliance score\n788\t   */\n789\t  private calculateComplianceScore(results: ComplianceCheckResult[]): number {\n790\t    if (results.length === 0) return 100;\n791\t\n792\t    const scores: Record<string, number> = {\n793\t      compliant: 100,\n794\t      partial: 50,\n795\t      non_compliant: 0,\n796\t      not_applicable: 100,\n797\t    };\n798\t\n799\t    const total = results.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (scores[r.status] ?? 0), 0);\n800\t    return Math.round(total / results.length);\n801\t  }\n802\t\n803\t  /**\n804\t   * Generate recommendations based on compliance results\n805\t   */\n806\t  private generateRecommendations(\n807\t    results: ComplianceCheckResult[],\n808\t    stats: { overdueCount: number; avgResponseDays: number },\n809\t  ): string[] {\n810\t    const recommendations: string[] = [];\n811\t\n812\t    // Based on compliance check results\n813\t    const nonCompliant = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'non_compliant');\n814\t    if (nonCompliant.length > 0) {\n815\t      recommendations.push(\n816\t        `Address ${nonCompliant.length} non-compliant requirement(s) immediately`,\n817\t      );\n818\t    }\n819\t\n820\t    const partial = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'partial');\n821\t    if (partial.length > 0) {\n822\t      recommendations.push(\n823\t        `Review and improve ${partial.length} partially compliant area(s)`,\n824\t      );\n825\t    }\n826\t\n827\t    // Based on data request stats\n828\t    if (stats.overdueCount > 0) {\n829\t      recommendations.push(\n830\t        `Process ${stats.overdueCount} overdue data request(s)`,\n831\t      );\n832\t    }\n833\t\n834\t    if (stats.avgResponseDays > 20) {\n835\t      recommendations.push(\n836\t        'Improve data request response time (currently averaging ' +\n837\t          `${stats.avgResponseDays} days, target is under 20 days)`,\n838\t      );\n839\t    }\n840\t\n841\t    return recommendations;\n842\t  }\n843\t\n844\t  /**\n845\t   * Generate executive summary\n846\t   */\n847\t  private generateExecutiveSummary(\n848\t    framework: ComplianceType,\n849\t    score: number,\n850\t    violationCount: number,\n851\t  ): string {\n852\t    const frameworkName = framework.toUpperCase();\n853\t    const status = score >= 90 ? 'strong' : score >= 70 ? 'adequate' : 'needs improvement';\n854\t\n855\t    let summary = `This report provides an assessment of ${frameworkName} compliance for the reporting period. `;\n856\t    summary += `Overall compliance score is ${score}%, indicating ${status} compliance posture. `;\n857\t\n858\t    if (violationCount > 0) {\n859\t      summary += `${violationCount} area(s) of non-compliance have been identified and require immediate attention. `;\n860\t    } else {\n861\t      summary += 'No critical compliance violations were identified. ';\n862\t    }\n863\t\n864\t    summary += 'Refer to detailed findings for specific requirements and recommendations.';\n865\t\n866\t    return summary;\n867\t  }\n868\t\n869\t  // ============================================================================\n870\t  // Data Inventory & Processing Records\n871\t  // ============================================================================\n872\t\n873\t  /**\n874\t   * Get data inventory (records of processing activities)\n875\t   */\n876\t  getDataInventory(): DataInventory[] {\n877\t    // In production, this would come from configuration/database\n878\t    return [\n879\t      {\n880\t        category: 'User Account Data',\n881\t        dataTypes: ['name', 'email', 'phone', 'address'],\n882\t        sources: ['User registration', 'Profile updates'],\n883\t        purposes: ['Account management', 'Communication'],\n884\t        retentionPeriod: 'Account lifetime + 7 years',\n885\t        legalBasis: 'Contract performance',\n886\t        thirdPartySharing: false,\n887\t        crossBorderTransfer: false,\n888\t      },\n889\t      {\n890\t        category: 'Farm Operations Data',\n891\t        dataTypes: ['sensor readings', 'production data', 'inventory'],\n892\t        sources: ['IoT sensors', 'User input', 'System automation'],\n893\t        purposes: ['Farm management', 'Analytics', 'Reporting'],\n894\t        retentionPeriod: '7 years',\n895\t        legalBasis: 'Contract performance',\n896\t        thirdPartySharing: false,\n897\t        crossBorderTransfer: false,\n898\t      },\n899\t      {\n900\t        category: 'Usage Analytics',\n901\t        dataTypes: ['page views', 'feature usage', 'session data'],\n902\t        sources: ['Application telemetry'],\n903\t        purposes: ['Product improvement', 'Support'],\n904\t        retentionPeriod: '2 years',\n905\t        legalBasis: 'Legitimate interest',\n906\t        thirdPartySharing: true,\n907\t        crossBorderTransfer: false,\n908\t      },\n909\t      {\n910\t        category: 'Support Communications',\n911\t        dataTypes: ['messages', 'tickets', 'attachments'],\n912\t        sources: ['Support system', 'Email'],\n913\t        purposes: ['Customer support', 'Quality assurance'],\n914\t        retentionPeriod: '3 years after resolution',\n915\t        legalBasis: 'Contract performance',\n916\t        thirdPartySharing: false,\n917\t        crossBorderTransfer: false,\n918\t      },\n919\t    ];\n920\t  }\n921\t\n922\t  // ============================================================================\n923\t  // Scheduled Tasks\n924\t  // ============================================================================\n925\t\n926\t  /**\n927\t   * Check for overdue requests and send reminders\n928\t   */\n929\t  @ScheduledJob({ name: 'compliance.check-overdue-requests', cron: CronExpression.EVERY_DAY_AT_9AM })\n930\t  async checkOverdueRequests(): Promise<void> {\n931\t    const overdue = await this.getOverdueRequests();\n932\t\n933\t    for (const request of overdue) {\n934\t      this.logger.warn(\n935\t        `Overdue data request: ${request.requestNumber} (${request.requestType}) - Due: ${request.dueDate}`,\n936\t      );\n937\t\n938\t      // In production, send notifications\n939\t      // await this.notificationService.sendOverdueRequestAlert(request);\n940\t    }\n941\t\n942\t    if (overdue.length > 0) {\n943\t      this.logger.warn(`Total overdue requests: ${overdue.length}`);\n944\t    }\n945\t  }\n946\t\n947\t  /**\n948\t   * Auto-generate monthly compliance reports\n949\t   */\n950\t  @ScheduledJob({ name: 'compliance.monthly-reports', cron: '0 0 1 * *' }) // First day of each month\n951\t  async generateMonthlyReports(): Promise<void> {\n952\t    this.logger.log('Generating monthly compliance reports...');\n953\t\n954\t    const now = new Date();\n955\t    const startDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1);\n956\t    const endDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 0);\n957\t\n958\t    await this.generateComplianceReport({\n959\t      complianceType: 'gdpr',\n960\t      reportPeriodStart: startDate,\n961\t      reportPeriodEnd: endDate,\n962\t      generatedBy: 'system',\n963\t      generatedByName: 'System',\n964\t      isAutoGenerated: true,\n965\t    });\n966\t\n967\t    this.logger.log('Monthly compliance reports generated');\n968\t  }\n969\t\n970\t  /**\n971\t   * Expire old download URLs\n972\t   */\n973\t  @ScheduledJob({ name: 'compliance.expire-download-urls', cron: CronExpression.EVERY_HOUR })\n974\t  async expireDownloadUrls(): Promise<void> {\n975\t    const result = await this.dataRequestRepository\n976\t      .createQueryBuilder()\n977\t      .update(DataRequest)\n978\t      .set({ downloadUrl: undefined })\n979\t      .where('downloadExpiresAt < :now', { now: new Date() })\n980\t      .andWhere('downloadUrl IS NOT NULL')\n981\t      .execute();\n982\t\n983\t    if (result.affected && result.affected > 0) {\n984\t      this.logger.log(`Expired ${result.affected} download URL(s)`);\n985\t    }\n986\t  }\n987\t\n988\t  // ============================================================================\n989\t  // Utility Methods\n990\t  // ============================================================================\n991\t\n992\t  private async generateRequestNumber(): Promise<string> {\n993\t    const year = new Date().getFullYear();\n994\t    const count = await this.dataRequestRepository.count({\n995\t      where: {\n996\t        createdAt: MoreThan(new Date(`${year}-01-01`)),\n997\t      },\n998\t    });\n999\t    return `DSR-${year}-${String(count + 1).padStart(5, '0')}`;\n1000\t  }\n1001\t\n1002\t  private getStatusChangeDescription(\n1003\t    oldStatus: DataRequestStatus | undefined,\n1004\t    newStatus: DataRequestStatus | undefined,\n1005\t  ): string {\n1006\t    if (!newStatus) return 'Status unchanged';\n1007\t    if (!oldStatus) return `Status set to ${newStatus}`;\n1008\t    return `Status changed from ${oldStatus} to ${newStatus}`;\n1009\t  }\n1010\t\n1011\t  private formatPeriod(start: Date, end: Date): string {\n1012\t    const format = (d: Date) =>\n1013\t      d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });\n1014\t    return `${format(start)} - ${format(end)}`;\n1015\t  }\n1016\t\n1017\t  private async logComplianceActivity(\n1018\t    action: string,\n1019\t    metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n1020\t  ): Promise<void> {\n1021\t    // Would integrate with ActivityLoggingService\n1022\t    this.logger.log(`Compliance activity: ${action}`, metadata);\n1023\t  }\n1024\t}\n1025\t\n1026\t// Helper for TypeORM Not operator\n1027\tfunction Not(value: unknown): unknown {\n1028\t  return { $not: value };\n1029\t}\n1030\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb78baa5-5a7e-414b-ac25-695f6e4fdcef","uuid":"f1d03b94-85f4-4635-9740-ab8f830a3abb","timestamp":"2026-09-19T23:10:43.937Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-c49a0b5a017f/apps/admin-api-service/src/security/services/compliance.service.ts","content":"  async getDataRequest(id: string): Promise<DataRequest> {\n    const request = await this.dataRequestRepository.findOne({ where: { id } });\n    if (!request) {\n      throw new NotFoundException(`Data request not found: ${id}`);\n    }\n    return request;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get all data requests with filters\n   */\n  async getDataRequests(options: {\n    page?: number;\n    limit?: number;\n    tenantId?: string;\n    requestType?: DataRequestType;\n    status?: DataRequestStatus;\n    complianceFramework?: ComplianceType;\n    startDate?: Date;\n    endDate?: Date;\n    overdue?: boolean;\n  }): Promise<PaginationResultV1<DataRequest>> {\n    const {\n      page = 1,\n      limit = 20,\n      tenantId,\n      requestType,\n      status,\n      complianceFramework,\n      startDate,\n      endDate,\n      overdue,\n    } = options;\n\n    const qb = this.dataRequestRepository.createQueryBuilder('request');\n\n    if (tenantId) qb.andWhere('request.tenantId = :tenantId', { tenantId });\n    if (requestType) qb.andWhere('request.requestType = :requestType', { requestType });\n    if (status) qb.andWhere('request.status = :status', { status });\n    if (complianceFramework) qb.andWhere('request.complianceFramework = :complianceFramework', { complianceFramework });\n    if (startDate) qb.andWhere('request.createdAt >= :startDate', { startDate });\n    if (endDate) qb.andWhere('request.createdAt <= :endDate', { endDate });\n    if (overdue) {\n      qb.andWhere('request.dueDate < :now', { now: new Date() });\n      qb.andWhere('request.status NOT IN (:...completedStatuses)', {\n        completedStatuses: ['completed', 'rejected'],\n      });\n    }\n\n    qb.orderBy('request.dueDate', 'ASC');\n    qb.skip((page - 1) * limit);\n    qb.take(limit);\n\n    const [data, total] = await qb.getManyAndCount();\n\n    return createStandardPaginatedResult<DataRequest>(data, total, page, limit);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Update data request\n   */\n  async updateDataRequest(\n    id: string,\n    data: Partial<{\n      status: DataRequestStatus;\n      assignedTo: string;\n      assignedToName: string;\n      completionNotes: string;\n      rejectionReason: string;\n    }>,\n    actorId: string,\n    actorName: string,\n  ): Promise<DataRequest> {\n    const request = await this.getDataRequest(id);\n\n    // Track changes in audit trail\n    const auditEntry = {\n      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      action: this.getStatusChangeDescription(request.status, data.status),\n      actor: actorName,\n      details: data.completionNotes || data.rejectionReason,\n    };\n\n    if (!request.auditTrail) request.auditTrail = [];\n    request.auditTrail.push(auditEntry);\n\n    // Update fields\n    if (data.status) {\n      request.status = data.status;\n      if (data.status === 'in_progress' && !request.processingStartedAt) {\n        request.processingStartedAt = new Date();\n      }\n      if (data.status === 'completed') {\n        request.completedAt = new Date();\n        request.completedBy = actorId;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (data.assignedTo) {\n      request.assignedTo = data.assignedTo;\n      request.assignedToName = data.assignedToName || null;\n    }\n\n    if (data.completionNotes) {\n      request.completionNotes = data.completionNotes;\n    }\n\n    if (data.rejectionReason) {\n      request.rejectionReason = data.rejectionReason;\n    }\n\n    return this.dataRequestRepository.save(request);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Verify requester identity\n   */\n  async verifyIdentity(\n    id: string,\n    verifiedBy: string,\n    verificationMethod: string,\n  ): Promise<DataRequest> {\n    const request = await this.getDataRequest(id);\n\n    request.identityVerified = true;\n    request.verifiedAt = new Date();\n    request.verifiedBy = verifiedBy;\n    request.verificationMethod = verificationMethod;\n\n    request.auditTrail?.push({\n      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      action: 'Identity verified',\n      actor: verifiedBy,\n      details: `Verification method: ${verificationMethod}`,\n    });\n\n    return this.dataRequestRepository.save(request);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Complete data request with delivery\n   */\n  async completeDataRequest(\n    id: string,\n    params: {\n      completedBy: string;\n      completionNotes: string;\n      deliveryFormat?: 'json' | 'csv' | 'pdf' | 'xml';\n      downloadUrl?: string;\n      downloadExpiresAt?: Date;\n    },\n  ): Promise<DataRequest> {\n    const request = await this.getDataRequest(id);\n\n    if (request.status === 'completed') {\n      throw new BadRequestException('Request is already completed');\n    }\n\n    request.status = 'completed';\n    request.completedAt = new Date();\n    request.completedBy = params.completedBy;\n    request.completionNotes = params.completionNotes;\n\n    if (params.deliveryFormat) {\n      request.deliveryFormat = params.deliveryFormat;\n      request.downloadUrl = params.downloadUrl || null;\n      request.downloadExpiresAt = params.downloadExpiresAt || null;\n    }\n\n    request.auditTrail?.push({\n      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n      action: 'Request completed',\n      actor: params.completedBy,\n      details: params.completionNotes,\n    });\n\n    return this.dataRequestRepository.save(request);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Record download of data request\n   */\n  async recordDownload(id: string): Promise<void> {\n    await this.dataRequestRepository.increment({ id }, 'downloadCount', 1);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get overdue requests\n   */\n  async getOverdueRequests(): Promise<DataRequest[]> {\n    return this.dataRequestRepository.find({\n      where: {\n        dueDate: LessThan(new Date()),\n        status: In(['pending', 'in_progress']),\n      },\n      order: { dueDate: 'ASC' },\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get request statistics\n   */\n  async getDataRequestStats(options: {\n    tenantId?: string;\n    startDate?: Date;\n    endDate?: Date;\n  }): Promise<{\n    total: number;\n    byStatus: Record<DataRequestStatus, number>;\n    byType: Record<DataRequestType, number>;\n    avgResponseDays: number;\n    overdueCount: number;\n    completionRate: number;\n  }> {\n    const { tenantId, startDate, endDate } = options;\n\n    const qb = this.dataRequestRepository.createQueryBuilder('request');\n    if (tenantId) qb.andWhere('request.tenantId = :tenantId', { tenantId });\n    if (startDate) qb.andWhere('request.createdAt >= :startDate', { startDate });\n    if (endDate) qb.andWhere('request.createdAt <= :endDate', { endDate });\n\n    const all = await qb.getMany();\n\n    // By status\n    const byStatus = {} as Record<DataRequestStatus, number>;\n    const statuses: DataRequestStatus[] = ['pending', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'rejected', 'expired'];\n    statuses.forEach((s) => {\n      byStatus[s] = all.filter((r) => r.status === s).length;\n    });\n\n    // By type\n    const byType = {} as Record<DataRequestType, number>;\n    const types: DataRequestType[] = ['access', 'deletion', 'portability', 'rectification', 'restriction'];\n    types.forEach((t) => {\n      byType[t] = all.filter((r) => r.requestType === t).length;\n    });\n\n    // Average response time\n    const completedWithTime = all.filter((r) => r.completedAt && r.createdAt);\n    const avgResponseDays = completedWithTime.length > 0\n      ? completedWithTime.reduce((sum, r) => {\n          const days = (r.completedAt!.getTime() - r.createdAt.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);\n          return sum + days;\n        }, 0) / completedWithTime.length\n      : 0;\n\n    // Overdue count\n    const now = new Date();\n    const overdueCount = all.filter(\n      (r) => r.dueDate < now && !['completed', 'rejected'].includes(r.status),\n    ).length;\n\n    // Completion rate\n    const completionRate = all.length > 0\n      ? (all.filter((r) => r.status === 'completed').length / all.length) * 100\n      : 0;\n\n    return {\n      total: all.length,\n      byStatus,\n      byType,\n      avgResponseDays: Math.round(avgResponseDays * 10) / 10,\n      overdueCount,\n      completionRate: Math.round(completionRate * 10) / 10,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Compliance Reporting\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * Generate compliance report\n   */\n  async generateComplianceReport(params: {\n    complianceType: ComplianceType;\n    reportPeriodStart: Date;\n    reportPeriodEnd: Date;\n    includedTenants?: string[];\n    generatedBy: string;\n    generatedByName: string;\n    isAutoGenerated?: boolean;\n  }): Promise<ComplianceReport> {\n    this.logger.log(`Generating ${params.complianceType} compliance report`);\n\n    // Gather metrics\n    const requestStats = await this.getDataRequestStats({\n      startDate: params.reportPeriodStart,\n      endDate: params.reportPeriodEnd,\n    });\n\n    const incidents = await this.incidentRepository.count({\n      where: {\n        createdAt: Between(params.reportPeriodStart, params.reportPeriodEnd),\n      },\n    });\n\n    const dataBreaches = await this.incidentRepository.count({\n      where: {\n        dataBreached: true,\n        createdAt: Between(params.reportPeriodStart, params.reportPeriodEnd),\n      },\n    });\n\n    // Run compliance checks\n    const complianceResults = await this.runComplianceChecks(params.complianceType);\n    const violations = complianceResults\n      .filter((r) => r.status === 'non_compliant')\n      .map((r) => ({\n        requirement: r.requirement.requirement,\n        description: r.details,\n        severity: 'high' as const,\n        remediation: r.remediation || 'Review and address non-compliance',\n      }));\n\n    const complianceScore = this.calculateComplianceScore(complianceResults);\n\n    // Generate recommendations\n    const recommendations = this.generateRecommendations(complianceResults, requestStats);\n\n    const report = this.reportRepository.create({\n      title: `${params.complianceType.toUpperCase()} Compliance Report - ${this.formatPeriod(params.reportPeriodStart, params.reportPeriodEnd)}`,\n      complianceType: params.complianceType,\n      reportPeriodStart: params.reportPeriodStart,\n      reportPeriodEnd: params.reportPeriodEnd,\n      includedTenants: params.includedTenants || null,\n      includesAllTenants: !params.includedTenants || params.includedTenants.length === 0,\n      totalDataRequests: requestStats.total,\n      completedDataRequests: requestStats.byStatus.completed || 0,\n      pendingDataRequests: (requestStats.byStatus.pending || 0) + (requestStats.byStatus.in_progress || 0),\n      avgResponseTimeDays: requestStats.avgResponseDays,\n      securityIncidents: incidents,\n      dataBreaches,\n      complianceScore,\n      violations: violations.length > 0 ? violations : null,\n      recommendations: recommendations.length > 0 ? recommendations : null,\n      executiveSummary: this.generateExecutiveSummary(params.complianceType, complianceScore, violations.length),\n      detailedFindings: {\n        complianceResults,\n        requestStats,\n        incidentSummary: { total: incidents, breaches: dataBreaches },\n      },\n      generatedBy: params.generatedBy,\n      generatedByName: params.generatedByName,\n      isAutoGenerated: params.isAutoGenerated ?? false,\n    });\n\n    return this.reportRepository.save(report);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get compliance report by ID\n   */\n  async getComplianceReport(id: string): Promise<ComplianceReport> {\n    const report = await this.reportRepository.findOne({ where: { id } });\n    if (!report) {\n      throw new NotFoundException(`Compliance report not found: ${id}`);\n    }\n    return report;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get all compliance reports\n   */\n  async getComplianceReports(options: {\n    page?: number;\n    limit?: number;\n    complianceType?: ComplianceType;\n    startDate?: Date;\n    endDate?: Date;\n  }): Promise<PaginationResultV1<ComplianceReport>> {\n    const { page = 1, limit = 20, complianceType, startDate, endDate } = options;\n\n    const qb = this.reportRepository.createQueryBuilder('report');\n\n    if (complianceType) qb.andWhere('report.complianceType = :complianceType', { complianceType });\n    if (startDate) qb.andWhere('report.reportPeriodStart >= :startDate', { startDate });\n    if (endDate) qb.andWhere('report.reportPeriodEnd <= :endDate', { endDate });\n\n    qb.orderBy('report.createdAt', 'DESC');\n    qb.skip((page - 1) * limit);\n    qb.take(limit);\n\n    const [data, total] = await qb.getManyAndCount();\n\n    return createStandardPaginatedResult<ComplianceReport>(data, total, page, limit);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Run compliance checks for a framework\n   */\n  async runComplianceChecks(framework: ComplianceType): Promise<ComplianceCheckResult[]> {\n    const requirements = this.getRequirements(framework);\n    const results: ComplianceCheckResult[] = [];\n\n    for (const req of requirements) {\n      const outcome = await this.checkRequirement(req);\n      // Stamped per check, not per run: the checks execute sequentially and\n      // some of them query, so one timestamp for the batch would be a claim\n      // about when the last one finished.\n      results.push({ ...outcome, checkedAt: new Date().toISOString() });\n    }\n\n    return results;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get compliance requirements for a framework\n   */\n  getRequirements(framework: ComplianceType): ComplianceRequirement[] {\n    switch (framework) {\n      case 'gdpr':\n        return GDPR_REQUIREMENTS;\n      default:\n        // Frameworks beyond GDPR (KVKK, SOC 2, ISO 27001) currently\n        // share the GDPR requirement set as their baseline because\n        // the platform's automated checks cover GDPR-equivalent\n        // surfaces. Distinct framework-specific requirement sets\n        // are tracked under each framework's own ADR (per the\n        // compliance-expert agent's framework-coverage matrix).\n        return GDPR_REQUIREMENTS;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check a single requirement.\n   *\n   * # Why the default branch returns 'partial' (COMPLIANCE-MEDIUM-002 cure)\n   *\n   * Pre-cure the `default:` branch returned `status: 'compliant'`\n   * for every requirement that did not have an explicit\n   * automatable check (gdpr-1, gdpr-4, gdpr-5, gdpr-6, gdpr-7,\n   * gdpr-8). The compliance score (`calculateComplianceScore`)\n   * averages 'compliant' as 100% — so 6 of the 8 GDPR\n   * requirements were inflating the score with unconditional\n   * 100s, producing a misleading 88% headline that an auditor\n   * would interpret as evidence of substantive automated\n   * coverage.\n   *\n   * The compliance-expert agent's tier hierarchy permits Tier-4\n   * (manual-attestation) checks but they MUST surface as\n   * `status: 'partial'` with an explicit attestation pointer —\n   * not as `'compliant'`. This cure restores tier-honesty:\n   *\n   *   - gdpr-2, gdpr-3 keep their automated checks (Tier-3).\n   *   - The other six requirements fall through to `'partial'`\n   *     with `details` pointing at the manual-attestation\n   *     evidence path. The 50%-weighted partial score deflates\n   *     the overall percentage to reflect actual automated\n   *     coverage rather than fictitious passes.\n   *\n   * The `calculateComplianceScore` weights are unchanged:\n   * compliant=100, partial=50, non_compliant=0, not_applicable=100.\n   * The cure is in WHICH bucket the default branch reports.\n   *\n   * Operators wanting a fully-automated coverage view should\n   * either implement Tier-3 checks for the remaining six (one\n   * per ADR / domain owner), or build an attestation pipeline\n   * that explicitly elevates each `partial` to `compliant`\n   * upon evidence-document review.\n   */\n  private async checkRequirement(req: ComplianceRequirement): Promise<ComplianceCheckOutcome> {\n    switch (req.id) {\n      case 'gdpr-2': { // Data Subject Rights\n        const pendingRequests = await this.dataRequestRepository.count({\n          where: { status: In(['pending', 'in_progress']) },\n        });\n        const overdue = await this.getOverdueRequests();\n        if (overdue.length > 0) {\n          return {\n            requirement: req,\n            status: 'non_compliant',\n            details: `${overdue.length} overdue data requests`,\n            remediation: 'Process overdue requests immediately',\n          };\n        }\n        return {\n          requirement: req,\n          status: 'compliant',\n          details: `Data request handling operational. ${pendingRequests} pending requests.`,\n        };\n      }\n\n      case 'gdpr-3': { // Breach Notification\n        const recentBreaches = await this.incidentRepository.count({\n          where: {\n            dataBreached: true,\n            reportedToAuthorities: false,\n            createdAt: MoreThan(new Date(Date.now() - 72 * 60 * 60 * 1000)), // Last 72 hours\n          },\n        });\n        if (recentBreaches > 0) {\n          return {\n            requirement: req,\n            status: 'non_compliant',\n            details: `${recentBreaches} unreported data breach(es) within 72 hours`,\n            remediation: 'Report breaches to supervisory authority immediately',\n          };\n        }\n        return {\n          requirement: req,\n          status: 'compliant',\n          details: 'No unreported breaches',\n        };\n      }\n\n      default:\n        // COMPLIANCE-MEDIUM-002 cure: requirements without an\n        // automated Tier-3 check report as 'partial' (not\n        // 'compliant') so the score honestly reflects automated\n        // coverage. `details` points at the per-requirement\n        // attestation path operators must populate to elevate\n        // the row to compliant — keeps the cure cooperative\n        // with the future evidence-document pipeline.\n        return {\n          requirement: req,\n          status: 'partial',\n          details:\n            `Automated check not implemented for requirement '${req.id}'. ` +\n            `Manual attestation required — see ` +\n            `docs/compliance/evidence/${req.id}.md.`,\n          remediation:\n            `Either implement an automated Tier-3 check for '${req.id}' ` +\n            `in checkRequirement(), or populate the attestation document ` +\n            `at docs/compliance/evidence/${req.id}.md and elevate via ` +\n            `the evidence-review pipeline.`,\n        };\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Calculate compliance score\n   */\n  private calculateComplianceScore(results: ComplianceCheckResult[]): number {\n    if (results.length === 0) return 100;\n\n    const scores: Record<string, number> = {\n      compliant: 100,\n      partial: 50,\n      non_compliant: 0,\n      not_applicable: 100,\n    };\n\n    const total = results.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (scores[r.status] ?? 0), 0);\n    return Math.round(total / results.length);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Generate recommendations based on compliance results\n   */\n  private generateRecommendations(\n    results: ComplianceCheckResult[],\n    stats: { overdueCount: number; avgResponseDays: number },\n  ): string[] {\n    const recommendations: string[] = [];\n\n    // Based on compliance check results\n    const nonCompliant = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'non_compliant');\n    if (nonCompliant.length > 0) {\n      recommendations.push(\n        `Address ${nonCompliant.length} non-compliant requirement(s) immediately`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    const partial = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'partial');\n    if (partial.length > 0) {\n      recommendations.push(\n        `Review and improve ${partial.length} partially compliant area(s)`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    // Based on data request stats\n    if (stats.overdueCount > 0) {\n      recommendations.push(\n        `Process ${stats.overdueCount} overdue data request(s)`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (stats.avgResponseDays > 20) {\n      recommendations.push(\n        'Improve data request response time (currently averaging ' +\n          `${stats.avgResponseDays} days, target is under 20 days)`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    return recommendations;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Generate executive summary\n   */\n  private generateExecutiveSummary(\n    framework: ComplianceType,\n    score: number,\n    violationCount: number,\n  ): string {\n    const frameworkName = framework.toUpperCase();\n    const status = score >= 90 ? 'strong' : score >= 70 ? 'adequate' : 'needs improvement';\n\n    let summary = `This report provides an assessment of ${frameworkName} compliance for the reporting period. `;\n    summary += `Overall compliance score is ${score}%, indicating ${status} compliance posture. `;\n\n    if (violationCount > 0) {\n      summary += `${violationCount} area(s) of non-compliance have been identified and require immediate attention. `;\n    } else {\n      summary += 'No critical compliance violations were identified. ';\n    }\n\n    summary += 'Refer to detailed findings for specific requirements and recommendations.';\n\n    return summary;\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Data Inventory & Processing Records\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * Get data inventory (records of processing activities)\n   */\n  getDataInventory(): DataInventory[] {\n    // In production, this would come from configuration/database\n    return [\n      {\n        category: 'User Account Data',\n        dataTypes: ['name', 'email', 'phone', 'address'],\n        sources: ['User registration', 'Profile updates'],\n        purposes: ['Account management', 'Communication'],\n        retentionPeriod: 'Account lifetime + 7 years',\n        legalBasis: 'Contract performance',\n        thirdPartySharing: false,\n        crossBorderTransfer: false,\n      },\n      {\n        category: 'Farm Operations Data',\n        dataTypes: ['sensor readings', 'production data', 'inventory'],\n        sources: ['IoT sensors', 'User input', 'System automation'],\n        purposes: ['Farm management', 'Analytics', 'Reporting'],\n        retentionPeriod: '7 years',\n        legalBasis: 'Contract performance',\n        thirdPartySharing: false,\n        crossBorderTransfer: false,\n      },\n      {\n        category: 'Usage Analytics',\n        dataTypes: ['page views', 'feature usage', 'session data'],\n        sources: ['Application telemetry'],\n        purposes: ['Product improvement', 'Support'],\n        retentionPeriod: '2 years',\n        legalBasis: 'Legitimate interest',\n        thirdPartySharing: true,\n        crossBorderTransfer: false,\n      },\n      {\n        category: 'Support Communications',\n        dataTypes: ['messages', 'tickets', 'attachments'],\n        sources: ['Support system', 'Email'],\n        purposes: ['Customer support', 'Quality assurance'],\n        retentionPeriod: '3 years after resolution',\n        legalBasis: 'Contract performance',\n        thirdPartySharing: false,\n        crossBorderTransfer: false,\n      },\n    ];\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Scheduled Tasks\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * Check for overdue requests and send reminders\n   */\n  @ScheduledJob({ name: 'compliance.check-overdue-requests', cron: CronExpression.EVERY_DAY_AT_9AM })\n  async checkOverdueRequests(): Promise<void> {\n    const overdue = await this.getOverdueRequests();\n\n    for (const request of overdue) {\n      this.logger.warn(\n        `Overdue data request: ${request.requestNumber} (${request.requestType}) - Due: ${request.dueDate}`,\n      );\n\n      // In production, send notifications\n      // await this.notificationService.sendOverdueRequestAlert(request);\n    }\n\n    if (overdue.length > 0) {\n      this.logger.warn(`Total overdue requests: ${overdue.length}`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Auto-generate monthly compliance reports\n   */\n  @ScheduledJob({ name: 'compliance.monthly-reports', cron: '0 0 1 * *' }) // First day of each month\n  async generateMonthlyReports(): Promise<void> {\n    this.logger.log('Generating monthly compliance reports...');\n\n    const now = new Date();\n    const startDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1);\n    const endDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 0);\n\n    await this.generateComplianceReport({\n      complianceType: 'gdpr',\n      reportPeriodStart: startDate,\n      reportPeriodEnd: endDate,\n      generatedBy: 'system',\n      generatedByName: 'System',\n      isAutoGenerated: true,\n    });\n\n    this.logger.log('Monthly compliance reports generated');\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Expire old download URLs\n   */\n  @ScheduledJob({ name: 'compliance.expire-download-urls', cron: CronExpression.EVERY_HOUR })\n  async expireDownloadUrls(): Promise<void> {\n    const result = await this.dataRequestRepository\n      .createQueryBuilder()\n      .update(DataRequest)\n      .set({ downloadUrl: undefined })\n      .where('downloadExpiresAt < :now', { now: new Date() })\n      .andWhere('downloadUrl IS NOT NULL')\n      .execute();\n\n    if (result.affected && result.affected > 0) {\n      this.logger.log(`Expired ${result.affected} download URL(s)`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Utility Methods\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  private async generateRequestNumber(): Promise<string> {\n    const year = new Date().getFullYear();\n    const count = await this.dataRequestRepository.count({\n      where: {\n        createdAt: MoreThan(new Date(`${year}-01-01`)),\n      },\n    });\n    return `DSR-${year}-${String(count + 1).padStart(5, '0')}`;\n  }\n\n  private getStatusChangeDescription(\n    oldStatus: DataRequestStatus | undefined,\n    newStatus: DataRequestStatus | undefined,\n  ): string {\n    if (!newStatus) return 'Status unchanged';\n    if (!oldStatus) return `Status set to ${newStatus}`;\n    return `Status changed from ${oldStatus} to ${newStatus}`;\n  }\n\n  private formatPeriod(start: Date, end: Date): string {\n    const format = (d: Date) =>\n      d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });\n    return `${format(start)} - ${format(end)}`;\n  }\n\n  private async logComplianceActivity(\n    action: string,\n    metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    // Would integrate with ActivityLoggingService\n    this.logger.log(`Compliance activity: ${action}`, metadata);\n  }\n}\n\n// Helper for TypeORM Not operator\nfunction Not(value: unknown): unknown {\n  return { $not: value };\n}\n","numLines":775,"startLine":256,"totalLines":1030}}}

\"feedback\": rows}\n\n\ndef _has_unverifiable_evidence(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], workspace_root: str | Path) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Plan 024 §C — True if any judge in the group cites an evidence ref that\n    positively does not resolve in the repo (``missing`` / ``invalid``). A ref\n    that exists but is unverified at a pinned sha (``worktree_candidate``) is\n    given the benefit of the doubt — this gate catches fabricated evidence, not\n    sha-pinning uncertainty, so it cannot flood escalation on a clean repo.\"\"\"\n    from .evidence_trust import classify_evidence_ref\n    for row in rows:\n        for ref in _optional_string_list(row.get(\"evidence_refs\")):\n            grade = classify_evidence_ref(\n                ref, workspace_root=workspace_root, context=\"consensus_evidence_gate\"\n            ).trust_grade\n            if grade in (\"missing\", \"invalid\"):\n                return True\n    return False","numLines":100,"startLine":640,"totalLines":1594}}}

if missing:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response missing required fields: {missing}\")\n    if envelope[\"$schema\"] != RESPONSE_SCHEMA:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response $schema must be {RESPONSE_SCHEMA!r}\")\n    if not isinstance(envelope[\"request_id\"], str) or not REQUEST_ID_RE.match(envelope[\"request_id\"]):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.request_id invalid: {envelope['request_id']!r}\")\n    claim_id = envelope[\"claim_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(claim_id, str) or not CLAIM_ID_RE.match(claim_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.claim_id invalid: {claim_id!r}\")\n    agent_id = envelope[\"agent_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or not AGENT_ID_RE.match(agent_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.agent_id invalid: {agent_id!r}\")\n    role = envelope[\"role\"]\n    if role not in INVOCATION_ROLES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.role unknown: {role!r}\")\n    status = envelope[\"status\"]\n    if status not in RESPONSE_STATUSES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.status unknown: {status!r}\")\n\n    matrix = envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"]\n    if not isinstance(matrix, list):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"agent-response.satisfaction_matrix must be a list\")\n    matrix_entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    matrix_ids: set[str] = set()\n    for idx, entry in enumerate(matrix):\n        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}] must be an object\")\n        item_id = entry.get(\"id\")\n        verdict = entry.get(\"verdict\")\n        if not isinstance(item_id, str) or not item_id.strip():\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id is required\")\n        if item_id in matrix_ids:\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id duplicate: {item_id}\")\n        matrix_ids.add(item_id)\n        if verdict not in SATISFACTION_VERDICTS:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].verdict {verdict!r} not in {SATISFACTION_VERDICTS}\"\n            )\n        if verdict in {\"blocked\", \"contradicted\"}:\n            note = entry.get(\"note\") or entry.get(\"rationale\") or \"\"\n            if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n                raise GovernanceError(\n                    f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].note required when verdict={verdict!r}\"\n                )\n            _check_banned_phrases(note, field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].note\")\n            evidence_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\", [])\n            _ensure_string_list(\n                evidence_refs,\n                field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].evidence_refs\",\n                allow_empty=False,\n            )\n        matrix_entries.append(entry)\n\n    rationale = envelope.get(\"rationale\")\n    if isinstance(rationale, str) and rationale.strip():\n        _check_banned_phrases(rationale, field=\"agent-response.rationale\")\n\n    # Plan 023 v3 §A-5 — envelope claim/agent identity bound to lease.\n    # When the caller supplies the leased claim+agent identity (which\n    # submit_claim_result does), reject any envelope whose claim_id or\n    # agent_id differs. Pre-fix a stitched envelope from a different\n    # lease (same request_id but different claim/agent) passed because\n    # only request_id was cross-checked.\n    if lease is not None:\n        leased_claim = lease.get(\"claim_id\")\n        leased_agent = lease.get(\"agent_id\")\n        if leased_claim is not None and envelope[\"claim_id\"] != leased_claim:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"envelope_claim_id_mismatch: envelope.claim_id=\"\n                f\"{envelope['claim_id']!r} does not match leased claim_id=\"\n                f\"{leased_claim!r}\"\n            )\n        if leased_agent is not None and envelope[\"agent_id\"] != leased_agent:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"envelope_agent_id_mismatch: envelope.agent_id=\"\n                f\"{envelope['agent_id']!r} does not match leased agent_id=\"\n                f\"{leased_agent!r}\"\n            )\n\n    if request is not None:\n        if envelope[\"request_id\"] != request.get(\"request_id\"):\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"agent-response.request_id {envelope['request_id']!r} does not match request \"\n                f\"{request.get('request_id')!r}\"\n            )\n        # Plan 024 v3 §H-4 — envelope.role must match request.role.\n        # Pre-fix only the membership check above (line 322) ensured\n        # the role was in REQUEST_ROLES; a request bound to role\n        # `domain_review` could accept a response with role\n        # `architectural_judgment` and pass cross-checks because the\n        # equality with request.role was never asserted. The\n        # downstream judgment_bridge + consensus path reads\n        # response.role to route the verdict, so a mismatched role\n        # silently misroutes the judgment.\n        request_role = request.get(\"role\")\n        if request_role is not None and envelope.get(\"role\") != request_role:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"response_role_mismatch_with_request: \"\n                f\"envelope.role={envelope.get('role')!r}, \"\n                f\"request.role={request_role!r}\"\n            )\n        # Every must_satisfy item must appear once in the matrix.\n        required_ids = {item[\"id\"] for item in request.get(\"must_satisfy\", [])}\n        unmet = required_ids - matrix_ids\n        if unmet:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix missing entries for must_satisfy ids: {sorted(unmet)}\"\n            )\n        extra = matrix_ids - required_ids\n        if extra:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix contains ids not in must_satisfy: {sorted(extra)}\"\n            )\n        if envelope.get(\"output_path\") and request.get(\"expected_output_path\"):\n            if envelope[\"output_path\"] != request[\"expected_output_path\"]:\n                raise GovernanceError(\n                    f\"agent-response.output_path {envelope['output_path']!r} differs from \"\n                    f\"request.expected_output_path {request['expected_output_path']!r}\"\n                )","numLines":140,"startLine":363,"totalLines":616}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011eFwx8ZEvhB92W6sr3TGtS","type":"tool_result","content":"460\tdef _check_agent_ref(\n461\t    ref: str,\n462\t    *,\n463\t    root: Path,\n464\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n465\t    checked: list[str],\n466\t    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n467\t    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n468\t) -> None:\n469\t    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n470\t    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n471\t    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n472\t    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n473\t    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n474\t    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n475\t    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n476\t    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n477\t    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n478\t    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n479\t    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n480\t    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n481\t        checked.append(ref)\n482\t        return\n483\t    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n484\t        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n485\t        if admissible:\n486\t            checked.append(ref)\n487\t            return\n488\t        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n489\t            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n490\t            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n491\t            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n492\t            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n493\t            errors.append({\n494\t                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n495\t                \"ref\": ref,\n496\t                \"reason\": reason,\n497\t            })\n498\t            return\n499\t    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n500\t    if parsed is None:\n501\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n502\t        return\n503\t    path, line = parsed\n504\t    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n505\t    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n506\t    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n507\t    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n508\t    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n509\t    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n510\t    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n511\t    try:\n512\t        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n513\t    except _GE as exc:\n514\t        msg = str(exc)\n515\t        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n516\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n517\t        else:\n518\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n519\t                           \"error\": msg})"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb78baa5-5a7e-414b-ac25-695f6e4fdcef","uuid":"600f772a-df24-4a4b-b966-8e10ae30004d","timestamp":"2026-09-19T23:11:55.974Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-c49a0b5a017f/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"def _check_agent_ref(\n    ref: str,\n    *,\n    root: Path,\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checked: list[str],\n    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n) -> None:\n    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n        checked.append(ref)\n        return\n    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n        if admissible:\n            checked.append(ref)\n            return\n        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": reason,\n            })\n            return\n    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n    if parsed is None:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n        return\n    path, line = parsed\n    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n    try:\n        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n    except _GE as exc:\n        msg = str(exc)\n        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n        else:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n                           \"error\": msg})","numLines":60,"startLine":460,"totalLines":774}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018EKhqgwEBnPFjo9KSiyuLj","type":"tool_result","content":"100\t    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n101\t    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n102\t    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n103\t    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n104\t    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n105\t    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n106\t\n107\t    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n108\t    \"\"\"\n109\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n110\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n111\t        return []\n112\t    errors: list[str] = []\n113\t    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n114\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n115\t        details = {}\n116\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n117\t    if not verdict_block:\n118\t        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n119\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n120\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n121\t        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n122\t        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n123\t        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n124\t        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n125\t        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n126\t        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n127\t        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n128\t        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n129\t        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n130\t        if (\n131\t            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n132\t            and verdict is None\n133\t            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n134\t        ):\n135\t            pass\n136\t        else:\n137\t            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n138\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n139\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n140\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n141\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n142\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n143\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n144\t    if not judge_id:\n145\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n146\t    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n147\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n148\t    return errors\n149\t\n150\t\n151\tdef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n152\t    *,\n153\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n154\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n155\t    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n156\t) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n157\t    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n158\t\n159\t    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n160\t    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n161\t\n162\t    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n163\t    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n164\t    the request envelope's optional fields; It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n189\t    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n190\t    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n191\t        arb_block = \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n206\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n207\t\n208\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n209\t    run_id = finding_id):\n212\t        raise GovernanceError(\n213\t            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n214\t            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n215\t        )\n216\t\n217\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n218\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n219\t _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n223\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n224\t    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n225\t    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n226\t    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n227\t    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n228\t    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n229\t    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n230\t    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n231\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n232\t    if not judge_id:\n233\t        raise GovernanceError(\n234\t            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n235\t            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n236\t        )\n237\t    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n238\t        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n239\t        # bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n243\t        )\n244\t\n245\t    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n246\t    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n247\t    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n248\t    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n249\t\n250\t    note = (\n251\t        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n252\t        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n253\t        or
