This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n\n    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return []\n    errors: list[str] = []\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n    if not verdict_block:\n        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if (\n            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n            and verdict is None\n            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n        ):\n            pass\n        else:\n            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n    return errors\n\n\ndef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n\n    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n\n    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n    the response.details).\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return None\n\n    # Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — one contract, two consumers: the executor's\n    # pre-submit gate checks exactly this list and releases instead of\n    # sealing an unfoldable accepted result; the bridge raises on it so a\n    # row that somehow slipped past still fails loudly, never half-folds.\n    contract_errors = validate_judge_response(request=request, response=response)\n    if contract_errors:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge contract violation: \" + \"; \".join(contract_errors)\n        )\n\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's legitimate non-verdict outcome\n    # folds through the SAME producer, ledger and idempotent escalation_id\n    # as the deterministic engine, so one sweep drains both lanes into one\n    # HUMAN_REQUIRED record. It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n        arb_block = _verdict_field(details)\n        arb_reason = arb_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if arb_block.get(\"verdict\") is None and arb_reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS:\n            from .feedback_store import record_consensus_uncertainty\n\n            row = record_consensus_uncertainty(\n                tool_id=str(request.get(\"tool_id\") or arb_block.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n                run_id=str(request.get(\"run_id\") or arb_block.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n                finding_id=str(request.get(\"finding_id\") or arb_block.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n                reason=str(arb_reason),\n                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                base_dir=base_dir,\n            )\n            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n        )\n    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n        )\n    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n\n    note = (\n        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n    )\n    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n\n    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n\n    row = record_operator_feedback(\n        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n        run_id=str(run_id),\n        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n        verdict=verdict,\n        severity=severity,\n        note=note,\n        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n        # predate the stamp.\n        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n        rationale=str(note),\n        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n        base_dir=base_dir,\n    )\n    return row","numLines":200,"startLine":100,"totalLines":388}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NaMYj6aoonWCrG5q7Y5dME","type":"tool_result","content":"2225\t            contract = \"tools/aria-poc/ci_executor_contract_proven.md\"\n2226\t            raise ClaudeCliUnavailable(f\"{exc}; see {contract}\") from exc\n2227\t        raise\n2228\t    # Plan ARIA-V7 §2g v2 + V7.10 envelope-extraction fix.\n2229\t    #\n2230\t    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n2231\t    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n2232\t    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n2233\t    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n2234\t    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n2235\t    # which fails:\n2236\t    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n2237\t    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n2238\t    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n2239\t    #\n2240\t    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n2241\t    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n2242\t    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n2243\t    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n2244\t    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n2245\t    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n2246\t    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n2247\t    #\n2248\t    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n2249\t    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n2250\t    # internal kernel identity fields.\n2251\t    if completed.stdout:\n2252\t        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2253\t            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n2254\t            request_id=request_id,\n2255\t            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n2256\t            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n2257\t            role=role,\n2258\t            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n2259\t            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n2260\t            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n2261\t        )\n2262\t        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n2263\t        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2264\t        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n2265\t        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2266\t        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n2267\t        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n2268\t        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n2269\t        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification\n2270\t        # between mint + record sites. request_envelope + tools_dir\n2271\t        # MUST both be supplied for the record to fire — None defaults\n2272\t        # preserve V8 backward-compat for callers that haven't migrated.\n2273\t        if (\n2274\t            completed.returncode == 0\n2275\t            and _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY is False\n2276\t            and request_envelope is not None\n2277\t            and tools_dir is not None\n2278\t        ):\n2279\t            _record_claude_cli_usage(\n2280\t                raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n2281\t                request_envelope=request_envelope,\n2282\t                tools_dir=tools_dir,\n2283\t                role=role,\n2284\t                request_id=request_id,"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb78baa5-5a7e-414b-ac25-695f6e4fdcef","uuid":"a96fe932-257c-4688-bdf9-3f24fa835707","timestamp":"2026-09-19T23:12:36.701Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-c49a0b5a017f/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"            contract = \"tools/aria-poc/ci_executor_contract_proven.md\"\n            raise ClaudeCliUnavailable(f\"{exc}; see {contract}\") from exc\n        raise\n    # Plan ARIA-V7 §2g v2 + V7.10 envelope-extraction fix.\n    #\n    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n    # which fails:\n    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n    #\n    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n    #\n    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n    # internal kernel identity fields.\n    if completed.stdout:\n        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n            request_id=request_id,\n            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n            role=role,\n            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n        )\n        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification\n        # between mint + record sites. request_envelope + tools_dir\n        # MUST both be supplied for the record to fire — None defaults\n        # preserve V8 backward-compat for callers that haven't migrated.\n        if (\n            completed.returncode == 0\n            and _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY is False\n            and request_envelope is not None\n            and tools_dir is not None\n        ):\n            _record_claude_cli_usage(\n                raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n                request_envelope=request_envelope,\n                tools_dir=tools_dir,\n                role=role,\n                request_id=request_id,","numLines":60,"startLine":2225,"totalLines":5265}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E4JXbmfdmS8SDmqaxwqzYL","type":"tool_result","content":"2496\tdef _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2497\t    *,\n2498\t    raw_stdout: str,\n2499\t    request_id: str,\n2500\t    claim_id: str,\n2501\t    agent_id: str,\n2502\t    role: str,\n2503\t    subagent_type: str,\n2504\t    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n2505\t    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n2506\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n2507\t    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n2508\t\n2509\t    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n2510\t    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n2511\t    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n2512\t    \"\"\"\n2513\t    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n2514\t    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n2515\t    if not agent_text:\n2516\t        agent_text = raw_stdout\n2517\t    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n2518\t\n2519\t    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n2520\t        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n2521\t        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2522\t        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n2523\t        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n2524\t        \"role\": role,\n2525\t        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n2526\t    }\n2527\t\n2528\t    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n2529\t    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n2530\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n2531\t    else:\n2532\t        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n2533\t        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n2534\t        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n2535\t        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n2536\t        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n2537\t        # the reply.\n2538\t        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n2539\t        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n2540\t        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n2541\t        if must_satisfy:\n2542\t            for item in must_satisfy:\n2543\t                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n2544\t                    continue\n2545\t                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n2546\t                if not cid:\n2547\t                    continue\n2548\t                synthesized.append({\n2549\t                    \"id\": cid,\n2550\t                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2551\t                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2552\t                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2553\t                })\n2554\t        if not synthesized:\n2555\t            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n2556\t            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n2557\t            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n2558\t            synthesized.append({\n2559\t                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n2560\t                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2561\t                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2562\t                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2563\t            })\n2564\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n2565\t\n2566\t    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n2567\t    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n2568\t        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n2569\t            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n2570\t    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n2571\t        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n2572\t\n2573\t    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n2574\t    # forensic context post-submission.\n2575\t    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n2576\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n2577\t        details = {}\n2578\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n2579\t    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n2580\t    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n2581\t    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n2582\t    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n2583\t    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n2584\t    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n2585\t    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n2586\t    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n2587\t    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n2588\t    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n2589\t    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n2590\t    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n2591\t    if dispatch_model:\n2592\t        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n2593\t    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n2594\t    usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n2595\t    if usage is not None:\n2596\t        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n2597\t    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n2598\t\n2599\t    return envelope\n2600\t\n2601\t\n2602\tdef _rejected_result_recorded(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n2603\t    \"\"\"True when the submit step's answer is a kernel-recorded REJECTED result row.\n2604\t\n2605\t    `aria_kernel agent submit-result` prints its result document on stdout and\n2606\t    exits 1 for anything but `accepted`. When that document carries a result\n2607\t    row with status `rejected`, the kernel has already appended the row: the\n2608\t    claim is terminal (`_assert_lifecycle_mutation_allowed`) and the request\n2609\t    derives REJECTED (`derive_request_state`, results dominate). Anything else\n2610\t    on a non-zero exit is a refusal before the row and leaves the claim live.\n2611\t    \"\"\"\n2612\t    text = submit_stdout or \"\"\n2613\t    start = text.find(\"{\")\n2614\t    if start < 0:\n2615\t        return False"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb78baa5-5a7e-414b-ac25-695f6e4fdcef","uuid":"a7719878-6197-4155-8026-c3a81a39cc9a","timestamp":"2026-09-19T23:12:46.231Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-c49a0b5a017f/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"def _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n    *,\n    raw_stdout: str,\n    request_id: str,\n    claim_id: str,\n    agent_id: str,\n    role: str,\n    subagent_type: str,\n    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n\n    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n    \"\"\"\n    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n    if not agent_text:\n        agent_text = raw_stdout\n    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n\n    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n        \"request_id\": request_id,\n        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n        \"role\": role,\n        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n    }\n\n    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n    else:\n        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n        # the reply.\n        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n        if must_satisfy:\n            for item in must_satisfy:\n                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n                    continue\n                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n                if not cid:\n                    continue\n                synthesized.append({\n                    \"id\": cid,\n                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n                })\n        if not synthesized:\n            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n            synthesized.append({\n                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n            })\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n\n    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n\n    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n    # forensic context post-submission.\n    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n    if dispatch_model:\n        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n    usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n    if usage is not None:\n        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n\n    return envelope\n\n\ndef _rejected_result_recorded(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when the submit step's answer is a kernel-recorded REJECTED result row.\n\n    `aria_kernel agent submit-result` prints its result document on stdout and\n    exits 1 for anything but `accepted`. When that document carries a result\n    row with status `rejected`, the kernel has already appended the row: the\n    claim is terminal (`_assert_lifecycle_mutation_allowed`) and the request\n    derives REJECTED (`derive_request_state`, results dominate). Anything else\n    on a non-zero exit is a refusal before the row and leaves the claim live.\n    \"\"\"\n    text = submit_stdout or \"\"\n    start = text.find(\"{\")\n    if start < 0:\n        return False","numLines":120,"startLine":2496,"totalLines":5265}}}

============================================================================\n183\t\n184\t@Injectable()\n185\texport class ComplianceService {\n186\t  private readonly logger = new RESPONSE_DEADLINES: Record<DataRequestType, number> = {\n  access: 30, // 30 days\n  deletion: 30,\n  portability: 30,\n  rectification: 30,\n  restriction: 30,\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Service\n// ============================================================================\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class ComplianceService {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(ComplianceService.name);\n\n  constructor(\n    @InjectRepository(DataRequest)\n    private readonly dataRequestRepository: Repository<DataRequest>,\n    @InjectRepository(ComplianceReport)\n    private readonly reportRepository: Repository<ComplianceReport>,\n    @InjectRepository(ActivityLog)\n    private readonly activityRepository: Repository<ActivityLog>,\n    @InjectRepository(SecurityIncident)\n    private readonly incidentRepository: Repository<SecurityIncident>,\n    @Inject(ScheduledJobRunner) readonly scheduledJobs: ScheduledJobExecutor,\n  ) {}\n\n  // ============================================================================\n  // Data Subject Requests\n  // ============================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * Create data subject request\n   */\n  async createDataRequest(params: DataRequestCreateParams): Promise<DataRequest> {\n    this.logger.log(`Creating ${params.requestType} request for ${params.requesterEmail}`);\n\n    const requestNumber = await this.generateRequestNumber();\n    const deadlineDays = RESPONSE_DEADLINES[params.requestType] || 30;\n    const dueDate = new Date();\n    dueDate.setDate(dueDate.getDate() +
